 = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C 
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000621- End*/

	(SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleId = prsTitleId AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + ') prsTitle,
	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') From CrmCompanyPersons_Lang ccpLn where ccpLn.CompanyPersonID =cmpP.CompanyPersonID AND ccpLn.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),
	(SELECT ISNULL(prsLName, '''') From CrmCompanyPersons_Lang ccpLn where ccpLn.CompanyPersonID =cmpP.CompanyPersonID AND ccpLn.LanguageID=' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + '),
	(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs_Lang JLN WHERE prsJobId = JLN.PersonJobID AND JLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as nvarchar(10)) + ') prsJob
	/*RZ14010302- Add cmpUsername and password*/
	,(SELECT [cmpUserName] FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) [cmpUserName]
    ,(SELECT [cmpPassword] FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) [cmpPassword]
	/*RZ14010302- End*/
FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cmpP
WHERE prsMobile <> '' ''
And IsNull(prsStopSendSms, '''') <> 1
) T

INNER JOIN 
( 
'
--
DECLARE @R VARCHAR(max) 
SELECT @R=Txt FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
--
SET @String = @String + 
' SELECT CompanyId, CompanyPersonId FROM (' 
+ 
@R 
+
') TT) ttt ON T.CompanyId = ttt.CompanyId 
		And T.CompanyPersonId = ttt.CompanyPersonId
'
--
--
RETURN((@String))
END







GO

/*ADBF93022012129408*/
/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspCheckDuplicateCompany]
(		
/*-- 
	EXEC CRMspCheckDuplicateCompany 0, 0, 't', 0, 2, 99, N'lack', 1033, 1065
*/
@Type int, @Id int, @Val NVarChar(50), @TitleID INT, @CityId INT, @UserID INT ,
/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LackOfShowText IS NULL OR @LackOfShowText = '')
	SET @LackOfShowText = N'عدم نمايش'

DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX), @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX), @from VARCHAR(MAX), @term VARCHAR(MAX)
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
/*--*/
DECLARE @Word NVARCHAR(50)
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, /*cmpName, 
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) Title,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') CompanyWithTitle,*/

		/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)cmpName,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)Title,
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)

		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId) City,
		ISNULL( (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL( (SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL(cmpAddress, '''') cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsFName, '''') prsName, 
		prsTel, prsTelII, prsFax, prsMobile, prsEmail, prsEmailII, cmpNumber,
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) ReferUser '

				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId '
		SET @term= 
				' Where ( REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like (''%'+@Val +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR  REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like (''%'+@Val +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like (''%'+@Val +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like (''%'+@Val +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) = '''+@Val + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) = '''+@Val + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
END
END


/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END






GO

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]
				WHERE [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId

				/*------------ CompanyPersons ------------*/
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany]
				SET [cmpMainPersonId] = NULL
				WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId

				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonPicture]
				WHERE [CompanyPersonId] IN(SELECT CompanyPersonID 
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
											WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId)

				/**/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
				 WHERE [CompanyPersonId] IN(SELECT CompanyPersonID 
											 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
											 WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId)
		
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
				WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId
		
				/*-------- CompanyChangeHistory ---*/
				/*RZ14011123- Add CrmCompanyChangeHistory*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang] 
				WHERE [CompanyChangeHistoryId] IN(SELECT CCH.[CompanyChangeHistoryId] 
												  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] CCH
												  WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId)
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] 
				WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId
				/*RZ14011123- End*/

				/*------------ Company ------------*/
				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
				WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId

				DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany]
				WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId
				/*--------------------------------------------*/
				SET @Result = '1'
			COMMIT
		/**/
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
			IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
				ROLLBACK
			SET @Result = '0'
		/**/
		END CATCH
	/**/
	END
	/*----------------------------------------------------------------*/
	UPDATE @TmpTable 
	SET [TypeError] = @Result
	WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId	

	/*--------------------------------------------*/
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @CompanyId
END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr
/*========================================================*/
/*============= Return list of company not deleted ===============*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[CompanyId] = C.CompanyID) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)	
	AND C.[CompanyID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[CompanyId] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [CompanyId]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
						ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END) CompanyName
		,(SELECT [TypeError] FROM @TmpTable TM WHERE TM.[CompanyId] = C.CompanyID) [TypeError]
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C  
	WHERE C.[CompanyID] IN(SELECT [CompanyId] FROM @TmpTable)
	AND C.[CompanyID] NOT IN(SELECT TM.[CompanyId] 
							 FROM @TmpTable TM
							 WHERE ISNULL([TypeError],'') = '1')
/**/
END
/*========================================================*/
/**/
END








GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspDyFormsReport]
(			
/*
	exec CRMspDyFormsReport 
*/

@FormId NVARCHAR(2000),
@FieldId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@Values NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@rptUserId INT,
@rptDate NVARCHAR(50),
@rptDay NVARCHAR(50),
/*RZ970829- Add parameters*/
@FromFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
@ToFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
@FromRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
@ToRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
@CompanyId NVARCHAR(4000),
@FirstRegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
@RegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
@CheckedStatusId NVARCHAR(2000),
/*RZ970829- End*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
/*RZ980404- Add parameters*/
,@MasterValueId INT = NULL,/*RZ980404- Add parameter MasterValueId*/
@FromCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
@ToCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
@CheckerUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
/*RZ980404- END*/
/*RZ14001130- Add VerificationWorkFlowId and new field type And Linke Type*/
@DyFormNumber NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@VerificationWorkflowId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
@VerificationWorkflowSetpId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
/*RZ14001130- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Term VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
, @QryII VARCHAR(MAX) = ''/*RZ990701- Optimize sp*/
/*Set Term*/
/*RZ970829- Use CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues , because add register Info and add EnableForUser and Roll*/
SET @Term = @Term + ' FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] F
						/*RZ970829- Use CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues  */
						INNER JOIN  [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] FFMV ON (F.[FormId] = FFMV.[FormId]) 
						INNER JOIN  [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV  ON (FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] = FFV.[MasterValueId]) 
						WHERE 1=1 
						AND (F.[FormId] IN (SELECT [FormId]      
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
									WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')  
							  OR F.[ParentFormId] IN (SELECT [FormId]      
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
									WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' ))   
						AND ISNULL(F.[IsActive],0) = 1 
						AND ISNULL(FFV.[IsDeleted],0) = 0 '
						/*RZ14001130- Add VerificationWorkFlowId and new field type And Linke Type*/
						+ ' AND FFMV.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' UNION  
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers] 
							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 27 ) '
						/*RZ14001130- End*/
/**/

IF(@FormId IS NOT NULL AND @FormId <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND F.[FormId] IN(' + @FormId + ') '		
END
/**/
/*RZ14010706- Add IsRepeatable and change type of save data and add formname and fieldName to values for print*/
/*IF(@FieldId IS NOT NULL AND @FieldId <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND FFV.FieldId IN(' + @FieldId + ') '
END
*/
/**/
IF(@Values IS NOT NULL AND @Values <> '')
BEGIN
	/*===================== Get Value =============================*/
	DECLARE @FieldVal NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @TmpTerm VARCHAR(MAX) = ''		
	WHILE LEN(@Values) > 0
	BEGIN
		IF PATINDEX('%|@Rpt^Frm@|%', @Values) > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @FieldVal = SUBSTRING(@Values, 0, PATINDEX('%|@Rpt^Frm@|%', @Values))
			/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @FieldValueTo NVARCHAR(MAX)
			DECLARE @FieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
			DECLARE @FieldType NVARCHAR(MAX)
			SET @FieldValue = NULL
			SET @FieldValueTo = NULL
			SET @FieldType = NULL
			/**/
			IF(CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal) > 0)
				SET @FieldValue = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, 1, CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal)-1)   
			ELSE 
				SET @FieldValue = @FieldVal
			/**/	
			IF(CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal) > 0)                                 
				SET @FieldValueTo = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, CHARINDEX('@:@', @FieldVal)+3, CHARINDEX('@T:T@', @FieldVal) - (CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal)+ 3))
			IF(CHARINDEX('@T:T@',@FieldVal) > 0) 
				SET @FieldType = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, CHARINDEX('@T:T@', @FieldVal)+5, Len(@FieldVal))
			/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
			IF(@TmpTerm <> '')
			BEGIN
				/*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' OR'*/
				
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' /*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*/
			END

			/*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*/
			/*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' FFV.MasterValueId IN( ' 
						 + ' SELECT FFVCDN.MasterValueId '
						 + ' FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFVCDN '
						 + ' WHERE 1 = 1 '*/
			/*RZ14010706- Add IsRepeatable and change type of save data and add formname and fieldName to values for print*/
			SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' FFV.MasterValueId IN( ' 
						 + ' SELECT DISTINCT FFVCDN.MasterValueId '
						 + ' FROM '
						 + ' (SELECT 
									 DFVV1.[FormId]     				
									,[MasterValueId]
									,DFVV1.[Value]
									,DFVV1.[FieldId]		
									,DFVV1.[FieldRepeatId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues]   DFV1 '			
			/**/
			IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
			BEGIN
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '	CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(ISNULL(DFV1.[Value],''[]'')) '
			END
			ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
			BEGIN
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '	CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(ISNULL((SELECT FFVLN.[Value] FROM [CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = DFV1.[FormFieldValueId] AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),''[]'')) '
			END
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '	CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(ISNULL(DFV1.[Value],''[]'')) '
			END
			/**/
			SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' WITH (
										[FormId] INT ''$.FormId'',
										[FieldId] INT ''$.FieldId'',
										[FieldRepeatId] BIGINT ''$.FieldRepeatId'',
										[Value] NVARCHAR(MAx) ''$.Value''
										)  
										AS DFVV1
								WHERE ISNULL(DFV1.[IsDeleted],0) = 0 									
								)FFVCDN '
						+ ' WHERE 1 = 1 '
			/*RZ14010706- End*/
			IF(@FormId IS NOT NULL AND @FormId <> '')
			BEGIN
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
					' AND FFVCDN.[FormId] IN(' + @FormId + ') '
			END
			/**/
			IF(@FieldId IS NOT NULL AND @FieldId <> '')
			BEGIN
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
					' AND FFVCDN.[FieldId] IN(' + @FieldId + ') '
			END
			
			/*RZ14001106- END*/
			/**/
			IF(@FieldType IS NOT NULL AND @FieldType <> '' AND 
				@FieldValueTo IS NOT NULL AND @FieldValueTo <> '')
			BEGIN
				/*IF(@FieldType = '2')
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' CAST((CASE FFV.Value WHEN '''' THEN ''0'' ELSE FFV.Value END) AS NUMERIC(18,3)) BETWEEN CAST(''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AND CAST(''' + @FieldValueTo + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) '
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
						' ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(FFV.Value,''/'',''''),''.'',''''),'':'','''')) BETWEEN ''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AND ''' + @FieldValueTo + ''') '
				END*/

				/*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*/
				IF(@FieldType = '2')
				BEGIN
					/*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND CAST((CASE FFVCDN.Value WHEN '''' THEN ''0'' ELSE FFVCDN.Value END) AS NUMERIC(18,3)) BETWEEN CAST(''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AND CAST(''' + @FieldValueTo + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) '*/
					/*RZ14020317- Add condition for number*/
					/*Add first condition because in some database not work report by number*/
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm 
					+ ' AND (FormFieldValueId IN(SELECT FFV_I.FormFieldValueId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV_I WHERE FFV_I.FormId IN(' + @FormId + '))'					
					+ 'AND (CASE WHEN ISNUMERIC(FFVCDN.[Value]) = 1 THEN  CONVERT(NUMERIC(18, 3), FFVCDN.[Value])  ELSE CAST(''0'' AS NUMERIC(18, 3)) END)  BETWEEN CAST(''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AND CAST(''' + @FieldValueTo + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) )'
					/*RZ14020317- End*/
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
						' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(FFVCDN.Value,''/'',''''),''.'',''''),'':'','''')) BETWEEN ''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AND ''' + @FieldValueTo + ''') '
				END
				/*RZ14001106- End*/
								
			END			
			ELSE IF(@FieldType = '8')
			BEGIN
				/*RZ990422- Modify checkbox*/
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@8', '')
				SET @FieldValue = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((@FieldValue) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				/*RZ990422- End*/
				/*RZ14010706- Add IsRepeatable and change type of save data and add formname and fieldName to values for print*/
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm  + ' AND ' + '''' + 
						 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(						 
												(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((FFVCDN.Value) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))						 						 
											,''|'')) '
				/*RZ14010706- END*/
				/*IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
				BEGIN
					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
						 @FieldValue + ' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(FFV.Value,''|'')) '*-/

					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '''' + 
						 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(FFV.Value,''|'')) '*-//-*RZ990422- Modify checkbox*-/

					/-*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*-/
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm  + ' AND ' + '''' + 
						 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(						 
												(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((FFVCDN.Value) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))						 						 
											,''|'')) '
					/-*RZ14001106- End*-/

				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
						 @FieldValue + ' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString((SELECT [Value] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFV.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),''|'')) '						*-/
					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '''' + /-*RZ990422- Modify checkbox*-/
						 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString((SELECT [Value] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFV.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),''|'')) '	*-/					

					/-*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*-/
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm  + ' AND ' + '''' + 
						 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString((SELECT 											 
											(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((FFVLN.[Value]) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))	
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFVCDN.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),''|'')) '						

					/-*RZ14001106- End*-/					
				END*/
			END	
			/*RZ14001130- Add VerificationWorkFlowId and new field type*/
			ELSE IF(@FieldType = '9' OR @FieldType = '10' OR @FieldType = '11')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*RZ14010706- Add IsRepeatable and change type of save data and add formname and fieldName to values for print*/
				/*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND (FFVCDN.Value = ' + @FieldValue + ') '*/
				/*RZ14010706- End*/
				/*RZ14020414- Add condition for select or checkbox or radio*/
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@9', '')
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@10', '')
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@11', '')
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND (FFVCDN.Value = ''' + @FieldValue + ''') '
				/*RZ14020414- End*/
				

				/*IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
				BEGIN					
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND (FFVCDN.Value = ' + @FieldValue + ') '										
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ((SELECT [Value] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFVCDN.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') = ' + @FieldValue + ') '
				END*/
			/**/
			END	
			/*RZ14001130- End*/			
			/*RZ14020414- Add condition for select or checkbox or radio*/
			ELSE IF(@FieldType = '6' OR @FieldType = '7')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@7', '')
				SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@6', '')
				SET @FieldValue = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((@FieldValue) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				

				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFVCDN.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) =''' + @FieldValue + ''') '
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14020414- End*/
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				/*RZ14010706- Add IsRepeatable and change type of save data and add formname and fieldName to values for print*/
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFVCDN.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '
				/*RZ14010706- End*/
				/*IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
				BEGIN
					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFV.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '*-/

					/-*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*-/
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFVCDN.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '
					/-*RZ14001106- End*-/

				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					/-*SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((SELECT [Value] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFV.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '*-/

					/-*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*-/
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
					' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((SELECT [Value] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] FFVLN WHERE FFVLN.[FieldValueId] = FFVCDN.FormFieldValueId AND FFVLN.[LanguageID] = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '
					/-*RZ14001106- End*-/
				END	*/																																									
			END														
			/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
			/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
			SET @Values = SUBSTRING(@Values,
									  LEN(@FieldVal + '|@Rpt^Frm@|') + 1,
									  LEN(@Values))

			/*RZ14001106- Change or to and in condition in DyFormFieldValues*/
			SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' )'
			/*RZ14001106- End*/

		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @FieldVal = @Values
			SET @Values = NULL        
		END
	END
	/*=============================================================*/
	IF(@TmpTerm <> '')
		SET @Term = @Term  + ' AND ( ' + @TmpTerm + ') '
	/*=============================================================*/		 
END

IF @CompanyId > '0'	
BEGIN
	IF @CompanyId = '-20'
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
		SELECT @txt = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
		AND TypeId = 1	
		/*--*/
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND FFMV.[CompanyId] IN (  	
		SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' + 
		@txt
		+ ' )t ) '	

		/*RZ14020325- Modify in report from company*/		
		IF(ISNULL(@txt,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
			DECLARE @CmpRptQry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@CmpRptPrjId INT = NULL,@CmpRptPrsId INT = NULL
			SET @CmpRptQry = ' 
			DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
				ProjectId INT,
				CompanyPersonId INT
			)
	
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			SELECT DISTINCT
				ProjectId,
				CompanyPersonId
			FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
			SELECT ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
			INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
			EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
			/*--------------------------------*/
			SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
			SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
			/*--------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId INT = NULL,@FrmHavePrj BIT = NULL,@FrmHavePrs BIT = NULL
			/*SET @CmpRptFrmSysPartId = (SELECT TOP (1) [CrmSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] WHERE [FormId] = @FormId)*/
			/*RZ14020912- Modify condition when use company report*/
			DECLARE @Parent_FormId INT 			
			SET @Parent_FormId = (SELECT TOP(1) FormId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] WHERE FormId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																								FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@FormId,',') 
																								WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
																				AND ISNULL(ParentFormId,0) = 0)			
			SET @CmpRptFrmSysPartId = (SELECT TOP (1) [CrmSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] WHERE [FormId] = @Parent_FormId)
			/*RZ14020912- End*/


			SELECT @FrmHavePrj = 1
			WHERE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId IN(12,13,20,30,40,50,51,60,61)

			SELECT @FrmHavePrs = 1
			WHERE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId IN(11,13,20,30,4050,51,60,61)
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrjId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@FrmHavePrj,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND  ISNULL(FFMV.[ProjectId],0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(ProjectId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t ) '
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@FrmHavePrs,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Term = @Term +
				' AND ISNULL(FFMV.[CompanyPersonId],0) IN (  	
				SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
				@txt
				+ ' )t) '

			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14020325- End*/
		/*-----------------------------------*/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND FFMV.[CompanyId] IN ( ' + @CompanyId +') '
	END		
END

DECLARE @CrmSysPart NVARCHAR(50), @FOSysPart NVARCHAR(50)
DECLARE @CrmSysPartId INT, @FOSysPartId INT


SET @CrmSysPart = (SELECT  TOP 1 [Part] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] SP WHERE SP.[SystemPartId] IN
							(SELECT [CrmSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] F WHERE F.[FormId] IN(SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@FormId,','))))

SET @FOSysPart = (SELECT  TOP 1 [Part] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] SP WHERE SP.[SystemPartId] IN
							(SELECT [FOSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] F WHERE F.[FormId] IN(SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@FormId,','))))
/**/
/*Set Permission for company*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @rptUserId
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @rptUserId
IF(@cmpDataPermision IS NOT NULL AND @cmpDataPermision <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @cmpDataPermision =' AND ([CompanyId] IN (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany c WHERE 1=1 ' + @cmpDataPermision + ') OR [CompanyId] IS NULL )' /*===> because in movemnet warehouse companyId is null*/	
END
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @rptUserId
/*Set Permission for other part*/
DECLARE @otherDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX) = ''

IF((@CrmSysPart IS NOT NULL AND @CrmSysPart <> '') OR (@FOSysPart IS NOT NULL AND @FOSysPart <> ''))
BEGIN
	/*	SET @otherDataPermision =' AND ( [MasterObjectId] IS NULL '*/
	/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser for person,project,conversation*/
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Prs' OR @FOSysPart = 'Prs')
	BEGIN
		/*Prs*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [CompanyPersonId] IN (SELECT Prs.CompanyPersonID
								FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons] Prs
								WHERE Prs.FirstRegisterUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23 ))) '

	END
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Prj' OR @FOSysPart = 'Prj')
	BEGIN
		/*Prj*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [ProjectId] IN (SELECT Prj.[ProjectId]
								FROM dbo.[CrmProjects] Prj
								WHERE Prj.prjStarterUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24))) '

	END

	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Cnvs' OR @FOSysPart = 'Cnvs')
	BEGIN
		/*Cnvs*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [ConversationId] IN (SELECT Cnvs.[ConversationId]
								FROM dbo.[CrmConversation] Cnvs
								WHERE 
								Cnvs.FirstRegisterUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)
								OR Cnvs.cnvsUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)
								OR Cnvs.cnvsReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)																
								)) '
	END
	/*RZ990504- END*/
	/**/
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Pord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Pord')
	BEGIN
		/*Pord*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT Pord.PreOrderId
								FROM dbo.[WsPreOrder] Pord
								WHERE Pord.pordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 ))) '

	END
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Ord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Ord')
	BEGIN
		/*Ord*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT ord.OrderId
								FROM dbo.[WsOrder] ord
								WHERE ord.ordReferUserId IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 ))) '
	END
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Bord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Bord')
	BEGIN
		/*Bord*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT bord.BuyOrderId
								FROM dbo.[WSBuyOrder] bord
								WHERE bord.bordReferUserId IN (SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 17 ))) '

	END
	IF( @CrmSysPart = 'WhDoc' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhDoc' OR 
			 @CrmSysPart = 'WhDraft' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhDraft' OR 
			 @CrmSysPart = 'WhMovement' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhMovement')
	BEGIN
		/*Wrh*/
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' [MasterObjectId] IS NULL OR ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT dc.DocId
								FROM dbo.[WSDoc] dc
								WHERE dc.FirstRegisterUserId IN (SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 18 ))) '
	END
	/**/	
	IF(@otherDataPermision <> '')
		SET @otherDataPermision = ' AND (  ' +  @otherDataPermision + ' ) '	
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
	SET @otherDataPermision = ''
END	
/*========================================================*/ 
EXECUTE ('SELECT * '  + @QryII + ' FROM ( ' + @Qry + @Term + ' ) tmp WHERE [ParentFormId] = 0 ' + @cmpDataPermision + @otherDataPermision +  ' ORDER BY [FirstRegisterDate],[DyFormNumber]')/*RZ14020221- Change OrderView*/

/*SELECT ('SELECT * '  + @QryII + ' FROM ( ' + @Qry + @Term + ' ) tmp WHERE [ParentFormId] = 0 ' + @cmpDataPermision + @otherDataPermision +  ' ORDER BY [FirstRegisterDate],[DyFormNumber]')*/


/**/
END

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspFillsmsTempNumberFromOrderIndicator]

	Exec CRMspFillsmsTempNumberFromOrderIndicator 3, '13960307', N'win', 1065, 1065 

*/
(
@UserId INT,
@FirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
@LanguageID INT ,
@DefaultLanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @IndicatorTermTxt NVARCHAR(max), @txt NVARCHAR(MAx)
SELECT @IndicatorTermTxt = Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 40 AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
Set @txt =
'Delete dbo.SmsTempMobileNumbers Where UserId = '+ CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+' 
INSERT INTO dbo.SmsTempMobileNumbers (
	Mobile,
	CompanyName,
	PersonName,
	Phone,

	UserId,

)

	prsMobile,
	/*ISNULL(
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId))
	, '''') + '' '' + 
	ISNULL(
		(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId)
	, '''') CompanyName,*/

	/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) CompanyName,
	/*RZ14000623- End*/	'


Set @txt = @txt + '	ISNULL(
		(SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId)
	, '''')+ '' '' + ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') PersonName, 
	ISNULL(prsPreTel, '''') + prsTel prsPhone,
	prsCompanyId,
	CompanyPersonId,
	ISNULL(LEN(prsMobile), 0),
	ISNULL(LEFT(prsMobile, 2), 0) MobilePreNum,'+

	/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
					(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId)) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) cmpName,*/

	/*RZ14000623- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/

		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpTitle, '
	Set @txt = @txt + ' (SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000623- End*/	
	
	(SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId) prsTitle,
	prsFName,
	prsLName,
	(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs WHERE prsJobId = PersonJobID) prsJob, ' +

@DefaultLanguageID INT

							ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
								ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')

-- [dbo].[CRMspInsertConversationFromReport] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ14010915*/
/*RZ14011125- Change CnvsDesc and CnvsTopicId*/
/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
Create procedure [dbo].[CRMspInsertConversationFromReport] 
(	
/*
	exec CRMspInsertConversationFromReport
	@CnvsReferUserId = 3,
	@CnvsRemindDate = NULL,
	@CnvsRemindDateTime = NULL,
	@CnvsDesc = N'test sp2',
	@CnvsDescLang = NULL,
	@CnvsTopicId = 34,
	@TxtUnknown = NULL,	
	@UserId = 3, 
	@Year1 = 30, 
	@Month1 = 9, 
	@RegisterDate = '2022-12-06',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '14010915',
	@AppType = 'dasti', 
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065
*/
@CnvsReferUserId INT,
@CnvsRemindDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@CnvsRemindDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@CnvsDesc NVARCHAR(MAX),/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
@CnvsDescLang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@CnvsTopicId INT,
@TxtUnknown NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT, 
@Year1 SMALLINT, 
@Month1 SMALLINT, 
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(10), 
@DefaultLanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@TxtUnknown,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @TxtUnknown = N'نا مشخص'	
/**/
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @Txt VARCHAR(MAX),@Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@ParmDefinition VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
SET @Txt = '0'
SELECT @txt = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE UserId = @UserId
AND TypeId = 1
/*------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @TmpTable Table(
	CompanyId INT,
	ProjectId INT	
)
IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Qry = ' 
	DECLARE @TmpTable Table(
		CompanyId INT,
		ProjectId INT	
	)
	
	INSERT INTO @TmpTable (CompanyId,ProjectId)
	SELECT DISTINCT
		CompanyID,
		ProjectId
	FROM ( ' +  @Txt + ' )t 
				
    SELECT CompanyID,ProjectId FROM @TmpTable '
			
	INSERT INTO @TmpTable(CompanyId,ProjectId)
	EXEC (@Qry)			
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@RegisterDay NVARCHAR(10)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST((CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)) AS NVARCHAR(8))
SET @RegisterDay = DATEPART(dw,@RegisterDate)
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*----------------------------*/
		DECLARE @CrsrCompanyId INT,@CrsrProjectId INT
		DECLARE CrsrAddPrjCnvs CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		/**/				
			SELECT 
				CompanyId,
				ProjectId	
			FROM @TmpTable
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrAddPrjCnvs
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddPrjCnvs INTO @CrsrCompanyId,@CrsrProjectId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			DECLARE @LastConversationId INT = NULL,@ConversationId INT = NULL,@cnvsProjectId INT = NULL,	
			@CrsrCnvsTopicId INT = NULL,@cnvsSubject NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@CrsrCnvsDesc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@cnvsPersonId INT = NULL,/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
			@cnvsReferDesc NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,@CnvsStatusId INT = NULL,
			@cnvsPriorityId INT = NULL,	@cnvsIsBookMark BIT = NULL,	
			@cnvsFOCmpCanSee BIT = NULL,@cnvsIsTicket BIT = NULL,
			@cnvsStatusProcessKindId INT = NULL,@CnvsStatusGroupId INT = NULL,
			@cnvsStatusResultId INT = NULL
			/*----------------------------*/
			SET @cnvsProjectId = @CrsrProjectId
			SELECT TOP (1) 
				 @LastConversationId = [ConversationID]				
				,@CrsrCnvsTopicId = [cnvsTopicId]
				,@cnvsSubject = [cnvsSubject]
				,@CrsrCnvsDesc = [cnvsDesc]
				,@cnvsPersonId = [cnvsPersonId]		
				,@cnvsReferDesc = [cnvsReferDesc]
				,@CnvsStatusId = [cnvsStatusId]
				,@cnvsPriorityId = [cnvsPriorityId]		
				,@cnvsIsBookMark = [cnvsIsBookMark]		
				,@cnvsFOCmpCanSee = [cnvsFOCmpCanSee]
				,@cnvsIsTicket = [cnvsIsTicket]								
				,@cnvsStatusProcessKindId = [cnvsStatusProcessKindId]
				,@CnvsStatusGroupId = [cnvsStatusGroupId]		
				,@cnvsStatusResultId = [cnvsStatusResultId]		
			FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation]
			WHERE cnvsProjectId = @CrsrProjectId
			AND ISNULL([cnvsIsCopy],0) = 0
			ORDER BY FirstRegisterDate DESC, [ConversationID] DESC
			/*----------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@LastConversationId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @CnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP(1) prjcnvsStatusGroupId FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE ProjectID = @CrsrProjectId)

				SELECT TOP(1) 
					@CnvsStatusId = [ConversationStatusID],
					@cnvsStatusProcessKindId = [ProcessKindId]
				FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus C
				WHERE GroupId = @CnvsStatusGroupId
				AND ISNULL(DeActive,0) = 0
				ORDER BY ISNULL(statusOrder, ConversationStatusID), GroupId
		
				SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
									FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
									WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @cnvsStatusId
									AND ISNULL([IsDefault],0) = 1
									ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsStatusResultId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
												FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
												WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @cnvsStatusId
												AND ISNULL([DeActive],0) = 0
												ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
				/**/
				END
				/**/
				SET @CrsrCnvsTopicId = @cnvsTopicId 
				SET @CrsrCnvsDesc = @CnvsDesc
				/**/
				SET @cnvsPersonId = (SELECT TOP(1) cmpMainPersonId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE CompanyID = @CrsrCompanyId)
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsPersonId = (SELECT TOP(1) [CompanyPersonID] 			
										 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] 
										 WHERE prsCompanyId = @CrsrCompanyId		
										 ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
						([prsCompanyId]
						,[prsLName]				
						,[UserId]
						,[Year1]
						,[Month1]
						,[RegisterDate]
						,[RegisterDatePersian]				
						,[ImportDate]
						,[ImportDateTime]
						,[prsFirstRegisterUserId]
						,[AppType]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate])
					VALUES
						(@CrsrCompanyId
						,@TxtUnknown												
						,@UserId
						,@Year1
						,@Month1
						,@RegisterDate
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDate
						,@UserId				
						,@AppType
						,@DefaultLanguageID
						,@UserId
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDate)
					SELECT @cnvsPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
					/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
					/******* Insert to lang table ********/
					IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) > 0 )
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
							([CompanyPersonID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[prsLName])
						SELECT 
							 @cnvsPersonId
							,L.LanguageID
							,@TxtUnknown
						FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
						WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
					/**/
					END

				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------*/
			/*RZ14011125- Change CnvsDesc and CnvsTopicId*/
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@LastConversationId,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @CrsrCnvsTopicId = ISNULL(@cnvsTopicId,@CrsrCnvsTopicId) 
				SET @CrsrCnvsDesc = ISNULL(@CnvsDesc,@CrsrCnvsDesc)
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14011125- End*/

			INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation]
				([cnvsProjectId]
				,[cnvsCompanyId]
				,[cnvsDate]
				,[cnvsDay]
				,[cnvsTime]
				,[cnvsDateTime]
				,[cnvsTopicId]
				,[cnvsSubject]
				,[cnvsDesc]
				,[cnvsPersonId]
				,[cnvsUserId]
				,[cnvsReferUserId]
				,[cnvsReferDesc]
				,[cnvsStatusId]
				,[cnvsPriorityId]
				,[cnvsRemindDate]
				,[cnvsRemindDateTime]
				,[cnvsIsBookMark]
				,[cnvsFirstRegisterUserId]
				,[cnvsFirstRegisterDate]
				,[cnvsFirstRegisterDateTime]
				,[UserId]
				,[Year1]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[cnvsFOCmpCanSee]
				,[cnvsIsTicket]
				,[cnvsStatusProcessKindId]
				,[cnvsStatusGroupId]        
				,[cnvsStatusResultId])
			VALUES
				(@cnvsProjectId
				,@CrsrCompanyId        
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDay
				,@RegisterTime
				,@RegisterDate
				,@CrsrCnvsTopicId
				,@cnvsSubject
				,@CrsrCnvsDesc
				,@cnvsPersonId
				,@UserId
				,@cnvsReferUserId
				,@cnvsReferDesc
				,@CnvsStatusId
				,@cnvsPriorityId
				,@cnvsRemindDate
				,@cnvsRemindDateTime
				,@cnvsIsBookMark
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@UserId
				,@Year1
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate        
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@cnvsFOCmpCanSee
				,@cnvsIsTicket       
				,@cnvsStatusProcessKindId
				,@cnvsStatusGroupId
				,@cnvsStatusResultId)

			SELECT @ConversationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/******* Insert to lang table ********/
			IF(ISNULL(@ConversationId,0) > 0 )
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@LastConversationId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@CnvsDescLang)) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
							([ConversationID]
							,[LanguageID]					
							,[cnvsDesc])
						SELECT 
							 @ConversationId
							,[LanguageId]
							,[Value]
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId',
							[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value'/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
			
						) AS JS
						WHERE JS.[LanguageId] <> @DefaultLanguageID
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@LastConversationId,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*RZ14011125- Change CnvsDesc and CnvsTopicId*/
					IF(ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@CnvsDescLang)) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
							([ConversationID]
							,[LanguageID]					
							,[cnvsDesc])
						SELECT 
							 @ConversationId
							,[LanguageId]
							,[Value]
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId',
							[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value'/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
			
						) AS JS
						WHERE JS.[LanguageId] <> @DefaultLanguageID
					/**/
					END /*RZ14011125- End*/
					ELSE
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
							([ConversationID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[cnvsSubject]
							,[cnvsDesc]
							,[cnvsReferDesc])
						SELECT 
							 @ConversationId
							,LanguageID
							,cnvsSubject
							,cnvsDesc
							,cnvsReferDesc
						FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
						WHERE ConversationID = @LastConversationId
					/**/
					END			
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddPrjCnvs INTO @CrsrCompanyId,@CrsrProjectId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrAddPrjCnvs
		DEALLOCATE CrsrAddPrjCnvs
		/*----------------------------*/		
		SELECT 1 AS Result							
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspInsertCrmSysDBTempInfoFormCompanyFilterData] | References permission/temp-filter machinery


/*RZ960213-Create SP*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspInsertCrmSysDBTempInfoFormCompanyFilterData]
(            
/* 
	exec CRMspInsertCrmSysDBTempInfoFormCompanyFilterData 3, 2
*/
@UserIdInDBTemp INT,
@TypeId INT,
@Txt NVARCHAR(MAX),
@FirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@LanguageID INT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------ BEGIN delete last temp for current user -----------------------*/
	DELETE FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
    WHERE UserId = @UserIdInDBTemp 
    AND TypeId = @TypeId
/*------------------------ END delete last temp for current user -------------------------*/
/*------------------------ BEGIN insert a new temp for current user ----------------------*/
	INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
           ([UserId]
           ,[TypeId]
           ,[TXT]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate])
     VALUES
           (@UserIdInDBTemp
           ,@TypeId
           ,@Txt
           ,@AppType
           ,@LanguageID
           ,@UserIdInDBTemp
           ,@FirstRegisterDatePersian
           ,GETDATE())
/*------------------------ END insert a new temp for current user ------------------------*/		
/**/
END

@PrjTitle NVARCHAR(100),

@UserId INT,

@Month1 SMALLINT,

)

BEGIN

END

DECLARE @Txt VARCHAR(MAX),@Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@ParmDefinition VARCHAR(MAX) = ''

AND TypeId = 1
/*------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @TmpTable Table(
	CompanyId INT	
)

/**/
	SET @Qry = ' 
	DECLARE @TmpTable Table(
		CompanyId INT	
	)
	
	INSERT INTO @TmpTable (CompanyId)
	SELECT DISTINCT
		CompanyID
	FROM ( ' +  @Txt + ' )t 
				
    SELECT CompanyID FROM @TmpTable '

    BEGIN TRANSACTION

				,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
				,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]

				,[RegisterDate]

				,@PrjcnvsStatusGroupId

				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END

			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*------------------ Conversation ---------------------*/

			IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@AddCnvs,0) = 1)

												FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus C
												WHERE [ConversationStatusID] = @CnvsStatusId)
		
				SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
									FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
									WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @CnvsStatusId
									AND ISNULL([IsDefault],0) = 1
									ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsStatusResultId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @cnvsStatusResultId = (SELECT TOP(1)  [ConversationStatusResultId]						
												FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusResult] CSR
												WHERE CSR.[ConversationStatusId] = @CnvsStatusId
												AND ISNULL([DeActive],0) = 0
												ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView], [ConversationStatusResultId]), [ConversationStatusId])
				/**/
				END
				/**/
				/*------------------------------------------*/
				SET @cnvsPersonId = (SELECT TOP(1) cmpMainPersonId FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE CompanyID = @CrsrCompanyId)
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsPersonId = (SELECT TOP(1) [CompanyPersonID] 			
										 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] 
										 WHERE prsCompanyId = @CrsrCompanyId		
										 ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
				END
				IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
						([prsCompanyId]

						,[Month1]

					VALUES
						(@CrsrCompanyId
						,@TxtUnknown												
						,@UserId
						,@Year1
						,@Month1
						,@RegisterDate
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDatePersian
						,@RegisterDate
						,@UserId

						,@DefaultLanguageID

					SELECT @cnvsPersonId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
					/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
					/******* Insert to lang table ********/
					IF(ISNULL(@cnvsPersonId,0) > 0 )
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
							([CompanyPersonID]
							,[LanguageID]

						SELECT 
							 @cnvsPersonId
							,L.LanguageID
							,@TxtUnknown
						FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
						WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END

				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation]

					,[cnvsDateTime]
					,[cnvsTopicId]					
					,[cnvsDesc]
					,[cnvsPersonId]
					,[cnvsUserId]
					,[cnvsReferUserId]					
					,[cnvsStatusId]
					,[cnvsRemindDate]
					,[cnvsRemindDateTime]					
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterUserId]
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterDate]
					,[cnvsFirstRegisterDateTime]
					,[UserId]
					,[Year1]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]

					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]

					,@CnvsDesc
					,@cnvsPersonId
					,@UserId

					,@cnvsStatusProcessKindId
					,@PrjcnvsStatusGroupId
					,@cnvsStatusResultId)
				SELECT @ConversationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				IF(ISNULL(@ConversationId,0) > 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@CnvsDescLang)) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmConversation_Lang]
							([ConversationID]
							,[LanguageID]					
							,[cnvsDesc])
						SELECT 
							@ConversationId
							,[LanguageId]
							,[Value]
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@CnvsDescLang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId',
							[Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value'/*RZ14020605- Add length to cnvsDesc 4000 to max*/
			
						) AS JS
						WHERE JS.[LanguageId] <> @DefaultLanguageID
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END				
			/**/
			END
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
			/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrAddPrjCnvs INTO @CrsrCompanyId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrAddPrjCnvs
		DEALLOCATE CrsrAddPrjCnvs
		/*----------------------------*/		
		SELECT 1 AS Result							
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[CRMspInsertSurveyHistoryFromCmpRep] | References permission/temp-filter machinery








/*NKR14000710*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[CRMspInsertSurveyHistoryFromCmpRep]
(		
/* 
	SELECT * FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 1

	CRMspTransferMobAndEmailFromMainPrsToCmpRep
	CRMspInsertSmsTempMobileNumbersFromGenerateAutoUP
	CRMspInsertEmailFromGenerateAutoUP


	exec [CRMspInsertSurveyHistoryFromCmpRep] 11, 2, 10, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, 30, 07, '2021-10-02 01:01:01', '14000710', 'dasti0710', 3, 1065, 1065
	exec CRMspTransferMobAndEmailFromMainPrsToCmpRep 3,1033,1065
*/
	@SurveyId INT,
	@DefSendSettingId INT,
	@SystemPartId INT,
	@ExpireDate DATETIME,
	@ExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@SendDate DATETIME,
	@SendDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	/**/
	@Year1 SMALLINT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
	@UserId INT,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL  
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@Txt VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @Cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
	/*========================================================*/
	SELECT @Txt=Txt FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
	IF(ISNULL(@Txt,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		SET @Txt = ' AND (CC.CompanyID IN ((SELECT CompanyId FROM (' + @Txt +') TT))) '
	END
	/*========================================================*/
	DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
			[CompanyId] INT,
			[cmpNumber] INT,
			[CompanyName] NVARCHAR(300), 
			[cmpMainPersonId] INT,
			[cmpMainPerson] NVARCHAR(150), 
			[cmpMainPersonMobile] NVARCHAR(100),
			[TypeError] INT,
			[SurveyHistoryId] INT
	)
	/*========================================================*/

	/*Create Columns*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Cols = 
			' CC.CompanyID,
			CC.[cmpNumber],
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) CompanyName,
			CC.[cmpMainPersonId],
			(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				) [cmpMainPerson],
			'
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Cols = 
			'
			CC.CompanyID,
			CC.[cmpNumber],
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(80)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(80)) + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(80)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(80)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) CompanyName,
			CC.[cmpMainPersonId],
			(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'''')  + '' '' +
					 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			 ) [cmpMainPerson],
			'
		/**/
	END

	SET @Cols = @Cols + '
			(SELECT P.[prsMobile] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] P WHERE P.[CompanyPersonID] = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]) AS [cmpMainPersonMobile],
			(
				CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpMainPersonId],0) = 0 THEN
						-1 /*NULL MAIN PERSON*/
						WHEN ISNULL((SELECT P.[prsMobile] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] P WHERE P.[CompanyPersonID] = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]),'''') = '''' THEN
						-2 /*NULL MOBILE*/
						WHEN ((LEN((SELECT P.[prsMobile] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] P WHERE P.[CompanyPersonID] = CC.[cmpMainPersonId])) <> 11 AND LEFT((SELECT P.[prsMobile] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] P WHERE P.[CompanyPersonID] = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]), 2) <> ''09'')) THEN
						-3 /*NOT TRUE FORMAT*/
					ELSE 
						NULL
				END
			) AS TypeError '

	/*========================================================*/

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'SELECT DISTINCT ' + @Cols + ' FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE 1 = 1 '

	/*EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)*/
	INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([CompanyId], [cmpNumber], [CompanyName], [cmpMainPersonId], [cmpMainPerson], [cmpMainPersonMobile], [TypeError])
	EXECUTE(@Qry + @Txt)

	/*Insert To SurveyHistory*/
	IF ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @TmpTable T WHERE T.TypeError IS NULL) > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		WHILE ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @TmpTable T WHERE T.TypeError IS NULL AND T.SurveyHistoryId IS NULL) > 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			DECLARE @CmpId INT = 0, @InsertedId INT = 0
			SET @CmpId = ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 T.CompanyId FROM @TmpTable T WHERE T.TypeError IS NULL AND T.SurveyHistoryId IS NULL), 0)
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@CmpId, 0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				INSERT INTO CrmSurveyHistory(
					[surhSurveyId],
					[surhDefSendSettingId],
					[surhSystemPartId],
					[surhSystemPartObjectId],
					[surhCmpId],
					[surhPrsId],
					[surhToMobileNumber],
					[surhReplyExpireDate],
					[surhReplyExpireDatePersian],
					[surhSendDate],
					[surhSendDatePersian],
					[FirstRegisterUserId],
					[FirstRegisterDate],
					[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
					[UserId],
					[Year1],
					[Month1],
					[RegisterDate],
					[RegisterDatePersian],
					[AppType],
					[LanguageID]
				)
				SELECT 
					@SurveyId,
					@DefSendSettingId,
					@SystemPartId,
					T.CompanyId,
					T.CompanyId,
					T.cmpMainPersonId,
					T.cmpMainPersonMobile,
					@ExpireDate,
					@ExpireDatePersian,
					@SendDate,
					@SendDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					@RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@UserId,
					@Year1,
					@Month1,
					@RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian,
					@AppType,
					@LanguageID
				FROM @TmpTable T
				WHERE T.CompanyId = @CmpId
				/**/
				SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/**/
				IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					/**/
					UPDATE @TmpTable
						SET SurveyHistoryId = @InsertedId
					WHERE CompanyId = @CmpId
					/**/
				END
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END
	
	/*Select @TmpTable For Create Link And Insert To Sms Tank*/
	SELECT 
		[CompanyId],
		[cmpNumber],
		[CompanyName],
		[cmpMainPersonId],
		[cmpMainPerson],
		[cmpMainPersonMobile],
		[TypeError],
		[SurveyHistoryId]
	FROM @TmpTable
	/*========================================================*/
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspLkHolooSelectCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ991016*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspLkHolooSelectCompany] 
/*
	exec [CRMspLkHolooSelectCompany]  3,'2019-12-1','2021-01-10','',1,10,1033,1065	
*/
(
@UserId INT,
@FDate DATETIME = NULL,
@TDATE DATETIME = NULL,
@Value NVARCHAR(250) = NULL,
@PageNumber INT = 1,
@PageSize INT = 50, 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX), @Term VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*set date permision*/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
IF(@FDate IS NOT NULL AND @TDATE IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ([FirstRegisterDate] BETWEEN '''+ CAST(@FDate AS NVARCHAR(200)) + ''' AND '''+ CAST(@TDATE AS NVARCHAR(200)) +''' )'
END

/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/	
	IF(ISNULL(@Value,'') <>'')
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([cmpName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
											NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
											REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
											NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')) '
	END
		
	SET @Qry = ';WITH CtePaging
		AS (
			SELECT 
				 [CompanyID]
				,[cmpNumber]
				,[cmpName]
				,[cmpTitleId]
				,(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId]) cmpTitle   
				
				,(
					ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL([cmpName],'''') + 
					ISNULL('' - '' + (SELECT TOP 1
										ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.[prsTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
										ISNULL([prsFName],'''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
									FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
									WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
									AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) ,'''')				
				)Company	
								
				,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL([cmpName],''''))	[CompanyName]
				,[cmpNationalCode]						          
				,[cmpEconomicCode]
				,[cmpTel]
				,[cmpFax]
				,(SELECT PR.Province FROM dbo.Province PR WHERE PR.ProvinceID = C.cmpProvinceId) [cmpProvince]
				,(SELECT CT.City FROM dbo.City CT WHERE CT.CityID = C.cmpCityId) [cmpCity]	
				,[cmpAddress]
				,[cmpZipCode]
				,[cmpKind]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsNationalCode
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsNationalCode]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsMobile
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsMobile]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsEmail
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsEmail]
				
				,[cmpCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCodeId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr]
				,Row_number() OVER(ORDER By [CompanyId]) AS RowNumber 	
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE 1= 1 ' + @Term + ' ' + @cmpDataPermision + '				
		) 
	/**/	
	SELECT 
		* , 
		(SELECT 
			CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') ) = 0					
			THEN
				Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'
			ELSE
				(Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') + 1
			END
		FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
	FROM   CtePaging 
	WHERE  RowNumber BETWEEN ( '+ CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' - 1 ) * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' + 1 AND '+ CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) 
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL OR @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN 
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@Value,'') <>'')
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
											NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
											REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
											NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')				
		OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([cmpName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
											NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
											REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
											NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')) '
	END
	
	SET @Qry = ';WITH CtePaging
		AS (
			SELECT 
				 [CompanyID]
				,[cmpNumber]
				,(SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')[cmpName]
				,[cmpTitleId]
				,(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') cmpTitle   
				
				,(
					ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') + 
					ISNULL('' - '' + (SELECT TOP 1
										ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.[prsTitleId] AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
										ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL([prsFName],'''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonId AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')
									FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
									WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
									AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) ,'''')				
				)Company	
				
				,(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),''''))	[CompanyName]
				,[cmpNationalCode]						          
				,[cmpEconomicCode]
				,[cmpTel]
				,[cmpFax]
				,(SELECT PR.Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PR WHERE PR.ProvinceID = C.cmpProvinceId AND PR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') [cmpProvince]
				,(SELECT CT.City FROM dbo.City_Lang CT WHERE CT.CityID = C.cmpCityId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') [cmpCity]	
				,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')[cmpAddress]
				,[cmpZipCode]
				,[cmpKind]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsNationalCode
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsNationalCode]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsMobile
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsMobile]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsEmail
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsEmail]
														          
				,[cmpCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCodeId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr]
				,Row_number() OVER(ORDER By [CompanyId]) AS RowNumber 	
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE 1 = 1 ' + @Term + ' ' + @cmpDataPermision + '				
		) 
	/**/	
	SELECT 
		* , 
		(SELECT 
			CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') ) = 0					
			THEN
				Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'
			ELSE
				(Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') + 1
			END
		FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
	FROM   CtePaging 
	WHERE  RowNumber BETWEEN ( '+ CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' - 1 ) * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' + 1 AND '+ CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) 
/**/
END
/*--*/
EXECUTE (@Qry)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspLkSelectCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*NKR991211*/
/*NKR14000219- Modify paging*/
/*RZ14000622- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14000725- Add fields and select by cmpCode or cmpNumber*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspLkSelectCompany] 
/*
	exec [CRMspLkSelectCompany]  3,'2000-12-1','2021-09-13','',0,0,1033,1065
*/
(
	@UserId INT,
	@FDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@TDATE DATETIME = NULL,
	@Value NVARCHAR(250) = NULL,
	@PageNumber INT = 1,
	@PageSize INT = 50, 
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX), @Term VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@TermI VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/*set date permision*/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
/*EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId*/
EXEC dbo.CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId  /*RZ14000622- Modify*/
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
IF(@FDate IS NOT NULL AND @TDATE IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ([FirstRegisterDate] BETWEEN '''+ CAST(@FDate AS NVARCHAR(200)) + ''' AND '''+ CAST(@TDATE AS NVARCHAR(200)) +''' )'
END

/*RZ14000725- Add fields and select by cmpCode or cmpNumber*/
IF(ISNULL(@Value,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @TermI = @TermI + ' OR C.[cmpNumber] Like ''%' + @Value  +'%'''
	SET @TermI = @TermI + ' OR C.[cmpCode] Like ''%' + @Value  +'%'''
/**/
END
/*RZ14000725- End*/
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/	
	IF(ISNULL(@Value,'') <>'')
	BEGIN
		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([cmpName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
											NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
											REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Value AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
											NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')) '*/

		/*RZ14000622- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((
											(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
												THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
														ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
												ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
															ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
													FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
													WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
													)				
											END)	
		)AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
