						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
				END) [CompanyName]
				/*RZ14000622- End*/ '
		SET @Qry =	@Qry + ' ,[cmpNationalCode]						          
				,[cmpEconomicCode]
				,[cmpTel]
				,[cmpFax]
				,(SELECT PR.Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PR WHERE PR.ProvinceID = C.cmpProvinceId AND PR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') [cmpProvince]
				,(SELECT CT.City FROM dbo.City_Lang CT WHERE CT.CityID = C.cmpCityId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') [cmpCity]	
				,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')[cmpAddress]
				,[cmpZipCode]
				,[cmpKind]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsNationalCode
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsNationalCode]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsMobile
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsMobile]
				,(SELECT TOP 1 prsEmail
				  FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons Cp 
				  WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId) [prsEmail] '
				/*RZ14000725- Add fields and select by cmpCode or cmpNumber*/
			SET @Qry =	@Qry + ' ,(SELECT CY.Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang CY WHERE CY.CountryID = C.cmpCountryId AND CY.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ') [cmpCountry]
				,[cmpPreTel]
				,[cmpTelII]
				,[cmpPreTelII]				
				,(SELECT [cmpAddressDesc] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')[cmpAddressDesc]
				,(SELECT [cmpDescription] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ')[cmpDescription]
				,[cmpWebSite]
				,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')
				  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				  WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				 )	[cmpMainPerson]

				 ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ISNULL([cmpNationalCode],'''')
					ELSE (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL(prsNationalCode,'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(prsNationalCode,'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [CompanyNationalCode]
				  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL(prsMobile,'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (ISNULL([cmpMobile],''''))					
						 END)															
					ELSE (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL(prsMobile,'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(prsMobile,'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [CompanyMobile]
				  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL(ISNULL(prsEmail,prsEmailII),'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (ISNULL([cmpEmail],''''))					
						 END)															
					ELSE (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL(ISNULL(prsEmail,prsEmailII),'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(ISNULL(prsEmail,prsEmailII),'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [CompanyEmail]
				  ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
						               ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
											 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [PersonName]
				 ,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')))
					ELSE (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''')									  
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '),'''') 
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)									
				END)  [CompanyTitle] 				  
				,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 1
					THEN (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT prsBirthDate
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 prsBirthDate
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [PersonBirthDate]
				,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 1
					THEN (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpMainPersonId],0) <> 0
						  THEN (SELECT prsBirthDateTime
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)
						  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 prsBirthDateTime
								 FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
								 WHERE CCP.prsCompanyId = C.[CompanyID]								 
								 ORDER BY ISNULL(CCP.prsOrderView, CCP.CompanyPersonID)		
								)					
						 END)																			
				  END) [PersonBirthDateTime]  '
			 /*RZ14000725- End*/										          
			SET @Qry =	@Qry + ' ,[cmpCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCodeId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCode]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerId]
				,[cmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr]
				,Row_number() OVER(ORDER By [CompanyId]) AS RowNumber 	
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
			WHERE 1 = 1 ' + @Term + ' ' + @cmpDataPermision + '				
		) 
	/**/	
	SELECT 
		*'
	/*NKR991211*/
	IF (ISNULL(@PageNumber, 0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ' , 
		(SELECT 
			CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') ) = 0					
			THEN
				Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +'
			ELSE
				(Count(*) / '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +') + 1
			END
		FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage'
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @Qry = @Qry + ', 0 AS AllPage'
		/**/
	END
	SET @Qry = @Qry + ' FROM  CtePaging ' /*+ 
						/-*NKR14000219*-/
						' ORDER BY RowNumber' */
	
	IF (ISNULL(@PageNumber, 0) > 0)
		SET @Qry = @Qry + 'WHERE  RowNumber BETWEEN ( '+ CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' - 1 ) * '+ 
							CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' + 1 AND '+ 
							CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) +' * '+ CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50))							
	/*NKR991211*/

	
	SET @Qry = @Qry + ' ORDER BY RowNumber' /*NKR14000219*/
/**/
END
/*--*/
/*SELECT @Qry AS '@QRY'*/
EXECUTE (@Qry)
/*SELECT (@Qry)*/

END

-- [dbo].[CRMspLoadCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*NKR14010810- Add Confition*/
/*NKR14010906- Add field*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspLoadCompany]
(
	/*
		exec CRMspLoadCompany 6047, 3, 'Search', '', 0, '', '', 0 , null, 1065, 1065

		exec [dbo].[CRMspLoadCompany] @CompanyId=0,@UserId=3,@Mode='',@DateParam='',@SpecialUserId=3,@SortFields='CompanyID',@tmpVal='',@Topcnt='100',@OnlyIds=NULL,@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065
	*/
	@CompanyId INT, 
	@UserId INT, 
	@Mode Varchar(50),
	@DateParam VARCHAR(10),
	@SpecialUserId INT, 
	@SortFields VARCHAR(1000),
	@tmpVal VARCHAR(1000),
	@Topcnt VARCHAR(10),
	@OnlyIds bit = NULL,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @selectCmpQueryTest VARCHAR(MAX), 
			@SearchTxtObjType NVARCHAR(200), @SearchTxtJsResult NVARCHAR(MAX)
	/**/
	SELECT 
		@SearchTxtObjType = TXTObjType,
		@SearchTxtJsResult = TXTJsResult
	FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 10 AND UserId = @UserId
	/**/
	IF(@LanguageID is null or @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSelectCompanyQueryText @CompanyId, @UserId, @Mode, @DateParam, @SpecialUserId, @SortFields, @tmpVal, @Topcnt, @selectCmpQueryTest OUT, @OnlyIds
		execute (@selectCmpQueryTest)
		/**/
	END
	ELSE 
	BEGIN
		/**/
		EXEC CRMspSelectCompanyQueryText_Lang @CompanyId, @UserId, @Mode, @DateParam, @SpecialUserId, @SortFields, @tmpVal, @Topcnt, @selectCmpQueryTest OUT, @OnlyIds, @LanguageID
		execute (@selectCmpQueryTest)
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END

Create  PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspMainSearch]
(
/*
	exec CRMspMainSearch 
	@Val = N's',
	@UserID = 3,
	@SearchType = 2,
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065,
	@SearchConditionMode = N'1,2'
*/
@Val NVARCHAR(200),
@UserID INT,
@SearchType INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL, /*ESP13950808*/
@SearchConditionMode NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL/*RZ14010506- Add SearchConditionMode*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*--*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010608- Replace space to % in search value*/
IF(ISNULL(@Val,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val,' ','%')
END
/*RZ14010608- End*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010506- Add SearchConditionMode*/
DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(max), @from VARCHAR(max), @Term VARCHAR(max)
IF(ISNULL(@SearchConditionMode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*------------------- Conditions ---------------*/
	SET @Term = ''
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1)) /*cmpName*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@Term = '')
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( '
		ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' OR '
		/*-------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010608- Replace space to % in search value*/
		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpname, '' '' ,'''') as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''*/
		/*RZ14010721- Convert character*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpname, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(4000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''
		/*RZ14010721- End*/

	/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 2)) /*cmpNumber*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@Term = '')
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( '
		ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' OR '
		/*-------------------------------*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' C.cmpNumber Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 3)) /*cmpPreTel,cmpPreTelII,cmpTel,cmpTelII*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@Term = '')
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( '
		ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' OR '
		/*-------------------------------*/
		SET @Term = @Term + '  C.cmpPreTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
							' OR C.cmpPreTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
							' OR C.cmpTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
							' OR C.cmpTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 4)) /*cmpPreTel*/
		BEGIN
		/**/

			ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/

		/**/
		END
		/**/

		BEGIN
		/**/

			ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/

		/*RZ14010721- Convert character*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpaddress, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(4000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''		
		/*RZ14010721- End*/

	/**/

	/**/
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 14)) /*prsLName,prsFName*/
	BEGIN

		/*-------------------------------*/

		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(P.prsLName, '' '' ,'''') as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + @Val  +'%''' +

		 '' ''+IsNull(C.cmpName, '''') cmpNameWithTitle, */
		/*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		 (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		   THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
		   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		 END) cmpName,

		 (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
				  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
		   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		 END) cmpNameWithTitle, 
		 /*RZ14000615- End*/
		C.cmpNumber,
		/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) cmpTitle, */
		/*RZ14000615- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/		 
		 (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
		   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''') 
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		 END) cmpTitle, 
		 /*RZ14000615- End*/
		C.cmpFame,
		(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = C.cmpGroupId) cmpGroup, 
		(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = 
		C.cmpSubGroupId) cmpSubGroup, 
		(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) 
		cmpGroupII, 
		(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType WHERE CompanyTypeID = C.cmpTypeId) 
		cmpType, 
		(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = 
		C.cmpPresentationId) cmpPresentation,
		C.cmpPreTel, 
		C.cmpTel, 
		C.cmpTelUpTo,
		C.cmpPreTelII, 
		C.cmpTelII, 
		C.cmpTelUpToII,
		C.cmpMobile, 
		C.cmpFax,	 
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) Country,  
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) Province, 
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId) City, 	
		C.cmpAddress, 
		C.cmpReferUserId,	
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) ReferUser, 
		P.prsLName,
		P.prsFName,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId), '''') 
		+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(P.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(P.prsFName, '''') PrsName, 
		P.prsTel, 
		P.prsTelDesc, 
		P.prsTelII, 
		P.prsMobile, 
		P.prsFax, 
		'' '' cnvsSubject,
		'' '' cnvsTopic,
		'' '' cnvsDesc,
		RTrim(Prj.PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, '''')) PrjTitle,
		P.CompanyPersonId prsId,
		prj.ProjectId prjId, 
		0 ConversationID 
		/*RZ971222- Add search prsNationalCode*/
	    ,P.prsNationalCode 
		,C.cmpNationalCode /*RZ990418- Add search cmpNationalCode*/
		/*RZ14010302- Add cmpCode and cmpUserName*/
		,C.[cmpUserName]
        ,C.[cmpCode]
		/*RZ14010302- End*/ '
		/*RZ14010526- Add fields*/
		Set @clmn = @clmn + ' 
		,C.cmpTelDesc
		,C.cmpTelIIDesc
		,(SELECT ISNULL(PJ.Jobs, '''') FROM CrmPersonJobs PJ WHERE PJ.PersonJobID = P.prsJobId) prsJob
		,P.prsPreTel
		,P.prsTelUpTo
		,'''' cnvsStatus 
		'
		/*RZ14010526- End*/
SET @from = 
	' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
			INNER JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P
					ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId
			INNER JOIN dbo.CrmProjects Prj 
					ON C.CompanyID = Prj.prjCompanyId		
	Where 1=1 
	/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser*/
	AND	P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
		(SELECT ' + convert(varchar(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
		SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + convert(varchar(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
	AND	Prj.[prjStarterUserId] IN 
			(SELECT ' + convert(varchar(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
			SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + convert(varchar(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
	/*RZ990504- End*/ '

Create  PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspMainSearch_Lang]
(			
/*
	exec CRMspMainSearch_Lang 
	@Val= N'p'
	,@UserID = 3,
	@SearchType = 3,
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@SearchConditionMode = N'1,2'

*/
@Val NVARCHAR(200),
@UserID INT,
@SearchType INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@SearchConditionMode NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL/*RZ14010506- Add SearchConditionMode*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*--*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010608- Replace space to % in search value*/
IF(ISNULL(@Val,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val,' ','%')
END
/*RZ14010608- End*/
/*----------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010506- Add SearchConditionMode*/
DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(max), @from VARCHAR(max), @Term VARCHAR(max)
IF(ISNULL(@SearchConditionMode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*------------------- Conditions ---------------*/
	SET @Term = ''
	IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1)) /*cmpName*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@Term = '')
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( '
		ELSE /*IF(@Term = '')*/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' OR '
		/*-------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010608- Replace space to % in search value*/
		/*SET @Term = @Term + ' REPLACE(CAST((SELECT REPLACE(cmpname, '' '' ,'''') FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang WHERE CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''*/
		/*RZ14010721- Convert character*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((SELECT REPLACE(cmpname, '' '' ,'''') FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang WHERE CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' 
							+ convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS NVARCHAR(4000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR
